import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import Nav from "@/components/Nav";
import MobileNav from "@/components/MobileNav";
import Hero from "@/components/Hero";
import { getAllPosts, getLatestPosts } from "@/lib/blog";
import { projects } from "@/content/projects";

/* ── Above-the-fold: direct import (need immediate render) ── */
/* Nav, MobileNav, Hero */

/* ── Below-the-fold: dynamic imports with SSR preserved ── */
/* ssr: true means server renders them into HTML for SEO,
   but client-side JS chunk loads separately = smaller initial bundle */

const Services = dynamic(() => import("@/components/Services"), {
  ssr: true,
  loading: () => (
    <section className="section" aria-busy="true">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="lazy-skeleton-grid" aria-hidden="true">
          <div className="lazy-skeleton-card" />

      </div>
    </section>
  ),
});

export default function Home() {
  const allPosts = getAllPosts();
  const recentPosts = getLatestPosts(6);

  const latestPost = recentPosts[0]
    ? {
        slug: recentPosts[0].slug,
        title: recentPosts[0].title,
        date: recentPosts[0].date,
      }
    : null;

  const archiveSource =
    recentPosts.length > 4
      ? recentPosts[4]
      : recentPosts[recentPosts.length - 1];
  const archivePost =
    archiveSource && archiveSource.slug !== latestPost?.slug
      ? {
          slug: archiveSource.slug,
          title: archiveSource.title,
          date: archiveSource.date,
        }
      : null;

  return (
    <>
      <Nav />
      <MobileNav />

      <main id="main">
        <Hero
          latestPost={latestPost}
          archivePost={archivePost}
          totalPosts={allPosts.length}
          totalProjects={projects.length}
        />
        <Services />
        <Process />
        <Portfolio />
        <About />
        <WhyMe />
        <Testimonials />
        <LatestBlogPosts />
        <Newsletter />
        <FAQ />
        <FinalCTA />
      </main>

      <Footer />
      <BackToTop />
      <RevealObserver />
      <SectionSwipeHandler />
      <SwipeHint />
      <PerfObserver />
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";

export default function GlassFilters() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const SCRIPT_ID = "shorakaei-liquid-glass-nav-engine";
    const existing = document.getElementById(SCRIPT_ID) as HTMLScriptElement | null;

    if (existing) return;

    const script = document.createElement("script");
    script.id = SCRIPT_ID;
    script.type = "module";
    script.src = "/liquid-glass-nav.js";
    script.async = true;

    script.onerror = () => {
      console.error(
        "Shorakaei Liquid Glass: /liquid-glass-nav.js could not be loaded."
      );
      script.remove();
    };

    document.head.appendChild(script);
  }, []);

  return null;
}

   MICRO-INTERACTIONS — Every touch feels alive

@media (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse) {
  button,
  a,
  [role="button"],
  .btn,
  .nav-order-button,
  .service-item,
  .portfolio-card,
  .project-type-option,
  .contact-method {
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
    transition:
      transform 120ms var(--ease-out),
      opacity 120ms var(--ease-out);
  }

  button:active,
  a:active,
  [role="button"]:active,
  .btn:active,
  .nav-order-button:active {
    transform: scale(0.96);
    opacity: 0.92;
  }
}

.btn {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  isolation: isolate;
}

.btn::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: radial-gradient(
    circle at var(--x, 50%) var(--y, 50%),
    color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 22%, transparent) 0%,
    transparent 45%
  );
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity 400ms var(--ease-out);
  z-index: -1;
}

.btn:hover::after {
  opacity: 1;
}

.btn-primary {
  transition:
    transform 250ms var(--ease-out),
    background-color 250ms var(--ease-out),
    box-shadow 400ms var(--ease-out);
}

.btn-primary:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.btn > span[aria-hidden="true"],
.btn > svg:last-child {
  transition: transform 250ms var(--ease-out);
}

.btn:hover > span[aria-hidden="true"],
.btn:hover > svg:last-child {
  transform: translateX(-4px);
}

.btn:active {
  transform: translateY(0) scale(0.98);
  transition-duration: 80ms;
}

.btn:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
  outline-offset: 3px;
  transition:
    outline-offset 200ms var(--ease-out),
    transform 250ms var(--ease-out);
}

a:not(.btn):not(.brand):not(.nav-link):not(.mobile-nav-item):not(.mobile-nav-cta):not(.portfolio-card):not(.service-item) {
  position: relative;
}

  transition:
    color 200ms var(--ease-out),
    background-color 200ms var(--ease-out);

/* ============================================================
   PERFORMANCE OVERRIDE — Non-composited padding fix
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Lighthouse flagged `padding-bottom` and `padding-top` on
   `header.site-nav` and `.nav-inner` as non-composited
   animations. These cause layout thrashing on every scroll
   direction change.

   Fix strategy:
   - Lock `.site-nav` padding to a single value (no change)
   - Remove padding from `.nav-inner` transitions entirely
   - Keep `transform`/`opacity` transitions on `.site-nav`
     so the smart-hide behavior still works (they are composited)
   - Keep `background`/`border`/`box-shadow` on `.nav-inner`
     so the "scrolled" state is still visually clear
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Loaded AFTER layout.css but BEFORE enhancements.css — the
   `!important` flags beat every downstream override.
   ============================================================ */

.site-nav,
.site-nav.is-scrolled {
  padding-block: 14px !important;
  transition:
    transform 380ms cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1),
    opacity 280ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) !important;
}

.site-nav .nav-inner,
.site-nav.is-scrolled .nav-inner {
  transition:
    background-color var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out),
    border-color var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out),
    box-shadow var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out) !important;
}
/* ============================================================
   NAV — MagneticButton vertical alignment fix
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   `MagneticButton` wraps children in two inline-block divs
   (.magnetic / .magnetic-inner). Because Persian text has a
   higher visual center inside its em box, the inline baseline
   alignment pushes the button slightly above the nav center.
   Fix: force the wrappers to be inline-flex with centered
   alignment so the pill itself becomes the aligned unit.
   ============================================================ */

.nav-actions .magnetic,
.nav-actions .magnetic-inner {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  vertical-align: middle;
}

.nav-actions .magnetic-inner {
  height: 100%;
}

.nav-order-button {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  height: 36px;
  padding-block: 0;
  line-height: 1;
  vertical-align: middle;
}

   با اثبات‌های فنی قابل تأیید.
   ============================================================ */

/* ============================================================
   FIX — Theme toggle icons
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   مشکل: در JSX از کلاس‌های `theme-icon-sun` و `theme-icon-moon`
   استفاده می‌شه، ولی layout.css فقط `icon-sun` و `icon-moon`
   رو استایل کرده بود. نتیجه: آیکن‌ها بدون رنگ و سایز درست.
   ============================================================ */

.site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 9px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  display: block;
  transition:
    opacity 500ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1),
    transform 700ms cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.4, 0.64, 1),
    color 500ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
}

/* Sun — light mode */
.site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon-sun {
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 1;
  transform: rotate(0deg) scale(1);
}

/* Moon — hidden in light mode */
.site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon-moon {
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0;
  transform: rotate(-100deg) scale(0.4);
}

/* Dark mode — flip visibility */
[data-theme="dark"] .site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon-sun {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: rotate(90deg) scale(0.4);
}

[data-theme="dark"] .site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon-moon {
  opacity: 1;
  transform: rotate(0deg) scale(1);
}

/* Mobile — smaller padding */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon {
    padding: 7px;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .site-nav .theme-toggle .theme-icon {
    transition: none;
  }
}

    600px circle at var(--hero-glow-x) var(--hero-glow-y),
    color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 12%, transparent) 0%,

/* ============================================================
   FINAL POLISH 2026 — ULTIMATE EDITION
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   基于 WCAG 2.2 AA/AAA、Core Web Vitals 2026、现代 CSS 特性、
   国际化（i18n/RTL）以及性能最佳实践构建。
   
   覆盖范围：
   1. 架构层（@layer）
   2. 零特异性重置（基于 :where()）
   3. 现代色彩系统（oklch, color-mix, relative color）
   4. 无障碍完成度（焦点、目标尺寸、辅助技术）
   5. 用户偏好媒体查询（动效、对比度、透明、数据）
   6. 现代布局（容器查询、:has()、逻辑属性）
   7. 视图过渡与滚动驱动动画
   8. 打印样式
   9. 国际化与双向文本（RTL）
   10. 微细节（选区、光标、滚动条）
   11. 性能优化（GPU 合成、内容可见性）
   12. 终极打磨（排版节奏、间距系统）
   ============================================================ */

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   1. 架构层 — 显式级联顺序
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   使用 @layer 定义明确的优先级，彻底告别 !important 和特异性战争[reference:0]。
   顺序：reset → tokens → base → layout → components → utilities → overrides
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer reset, tokens, base, layout, components, utilities, overrides;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   2. 零特异性重置 — 基于 :where()
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   所有规则包裹在 :where() 中，特异性为 0，确保任何自定义样式都能轻松覆盖[reference:1]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer reset {
  /* 盒模型与尺寸 */
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    border: 0 solid currentColor; /* 确保边框可见于强制颜色模式[reference:2] */
  }

  /* 文档基础 */
  html {
    -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;
    -moz-text-size-adjust: 100%;
    text-size-adjust: 100%;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    font-kerning: normal;
    font-variant-ligatures: common-ligatures contextual;
    font-synthesis: none;
    scrollbar-gutter: stable; /* 防止滚动条出现/消失时的布局抖动[reference:3] */
    overflow-x: clip; /* 兼容 position: sticky 的水平安全裁剪[reference:4] */
    tab-size: 2;
  }

  /* 正文 */
  body {
    min-height: 100svh;
    line-height: 1.5;
    text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }

  /* 媒体元素 */
  :where(img, picture, video, canvas, svg) {
    display: block;
    max-inline-size: 100%;
    max-block-size: 100%;
  }

  /* 表单元素 */
  :where(input, button, textarea, select) {
    font: inherit;
    color: inherit;
    background: none;
    border: none;
    outline: none;
    appearance: none;
    -webkit-appearance: none;
  }

  /* 按钮和链接 */
  :where(button, [role="button"]) {
    cursor: pointer;
    background: none;
    text-align: inherit;
  }

  :where(a) {
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
  }

  /* 列表 */
  :where(ul, ol) {
    list-style: none;
  }

  /* 标题和段落 */
  :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, p) {
    font-size: inherit;
    font-weight: inherit;
    text-wrap: balance; /* 标题自动平衡 */
  }

  :where(p) {
    text-wrap: pretty; /* 段落避免孤行 */
  }

  /* 表格 */
  :where(table) {
    border-collapse: collapse;
    border-spacing: 0;
    text-indent: 0;
  }

  /* 代码 */
  :where(pre, code, kbd, samp) {
    font-family: var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace);
    font-size: 0.9em;
    direction: ltr; /* 代码始终 LTR */
    unicode-bidi: isolate;
  }

  /* 引用 */
  :where(blockquote, q) {
    quotes: none;
  }

  /* 细节/摘要 */
  :where(details) {
    display: block;
  }

  :where(summary) {
    cursor: pointer;
    list-style: none;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  }

  :where(summary)::-webkit-details-marker {
    display: none;
  }

  :where(summary)::marker {
    content: "";
  }

  /* 强制颜色模式下的无障碍基础 */
  @media (forced-colors: active) {
    :where(button, input, textarea, select, a, [role="button"]) {
      border: 1px solid ButtonText;
    }
    :where(img) {
      forced-color-adjust: auto;
    }
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  :root {

    --font-mono: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", "JetBrains Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;

    --shadow-md: 0 4px 12px -4px oklch(0 0 0 / 0.5);
    --shadow-lg: 0 12px 32px -12px oklch(0 0 0 / 0.6);
    --shadow-xl: 0 24px 64px -24px oklch(0 0 0 / 0.8);
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   4. 现代色彩函数 — 动态与对比
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   使用相对颜色语法和 color-contrast() 实现自适应对比度[reference:8]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  /* ─── 基于 Accent 的派生色 ─── */
  .derived-accent {
    color: oklch(from var(--accent) l c h); /* 原始 */
    color: oklch(from var(--accent) calc(l + 0.15) c h); /* 变亮 */
    color: oklch(from var(--accent) calc(l - 0.15) c h); /* 变暗 */
    color: oklch(from var(--accent) l calc(c * 0.5) h); /* 去饱和 */
  }

  /* ─── 自适应对比度文本（现代浏览器） ─── */
  @supports (color: contrast-color(red)) {
    .adaptive-text {
      color: contrast-color(var(--surface));
    }
  }

  /* ─── 回退：使用 color-mix 计算亮度差 ─── */
  .adaptive-text-fallback {
    color: light-dark(var(--ink), var(--paper));
  }

  /* ─── 动态透明度（不依赖预定义变量） ─── */
  .tinted {
    background: color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 15%, transparent);
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 30%, transparent);
  }

  /* ─── 色相旋转动画（使用 @property 实现平滑插值）[reference:9] ─── */
  @property --hue-rotate {
    syntax: "<angle>";
    inherits: false;
    initial-value: 0deg;
  }

  .hue-cycle {
    transition: --hue-rotate 1s var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  .hue-cycle:hover {
    --hue-rotate: 360deg;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   5. 无障碍完成度 — WCAG 2.2 AA/AAA
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   焦点、目标尺寸、辅助功能，全部到位。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  /* ─── 焦点可见性（2.4.7 Focus Visible） ─── */
  :where(
    a,
    button,
    input,
    textarea,
    select,
    [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"]),
    [role="button"],
    [role="link"],
    summary,
    details
  ):focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--accent);
    outline-offset: 3px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
    transition: outline-offset var(--dur-fast) var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  /* ─── 焦点不被遮挡（2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured）[reference:10] ─── */
  :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, [id]) {
    scroll-margin-block-start: 6rem; /* 为粘性头部预留空间 */
    scroll-margin-block-end: 2rem;
  }

  /* ─── 触摸目标尺寸（2.5.8 Target Size）[reference:11] ─── */
  @media (pointer: coarse) {
    :where(
      button,
      a,
      [role="button"],
      [role="link"],
      input[type="checkbox"],
      input[type="radio"],
      summary,
      .oform-option,
      .oform-contact
    ) {
      min-block-size: 44px;
      min-inline-size: 44px;
    }

    /* 内联文本中的链接除外 */
    :where(p, li, blockquote, dd) :where(a) {
      min-block-size: auto;
      min-inline-size: auto;
      padding: 2px 4px;
      margin: -2px -4px;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 拖拽替代（2.5.7 Dragging Movements）[reference:12] ─── */
  /* 对于任何可拖拽元素，提供键盘/点击替代方案 */
  [draggable="true"] {
    cursor: grab;
  }

  [draggable="true"]:active {
    cursor: grabbing;
  }

  /* 确保滑块有数字输入回退 */
  .slider-with-fallback {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-2);
  }

  /* ─── 跳过链接（2.4.1 Bypass Blocks） ─── */
  .skip-link {
    position: fixed;
    inset-block-start: -100px;
    inset-inline-start: 1rem;
    z-index: 10000;
    padding: 0.75rem 1.25rem;
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-xl);
    transition: inset-block-start var(--dur-normal) var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  .skip-link:focus,
  .skip-link:focus-visible {
    inset-block-start: 1rem;
    outline: 3px solid var(--accent);
    outline-offset: 3px;
  }

  /* ─── 屏幕阅读器专用（1.3.1 Info and Relationships） ─── */
  .sr-only {
    position: absolute !important;
    inline-size: 1px !important;
    block-size: 1px !important;
    padding: 0 !important;
    margin: -1px !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    clip-path: inset(50%) !important;
    white-space: nowrap !important;
    border: 0 !important;
  }

  .sr-only-focusable:focus,
  .sr-only-focusable:focus-visible {
    position: static !important;
    inline-size: auto !important;
    block-size: auto !important;
    padding: inherit !important;
    margin: inherit !important;
    overflow: visible !important;
    clip-path: none !important;
    white-space: normal !important;
  }

  /* ─── ARIA 状态样式 ─── */
  [aria-busy="true"] {
    cursor: progress;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  [aria-disabled="true"] {
    cursor: not-allowed;
    opacity: 0.5;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  /* ─── 减少动态效果（2.3.3 Animation from Interactions） ─── */
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
      animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
      animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
      transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
      scroll-behavior: auto !important;
    }

    html {
      scroll-behavior: auto !important;
    }

    :where(.reveal, .reveal-fade, .reveal-scale) {
      opacity: 1 !important;
      transform: none !important;
      filter: none !important;
      transition: none !important;
    }
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   6. 用户偏好媒体查询 — 完整的无障碍矩阵
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   尊重操作系统层面的每一个用户偏好[reference:13][reference:14]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer utilities {
  /* ─── 颜色方案偏好（自动，由主题系统处理） ─── */
  :root {
    color-scheme: light dark;
  }

  /* ─── 强制颜色模式（Windows 高对比度）[reference:15] ─── */
  @media (forced-colors: active) {
    * {
      forced-color-adjust: auto;
    }

    :where(.button, .btn, button, a, input, textarea, select) {
      border: 1px solid ButtonText;
    }

    :where(.button-primary, .btn-primary, .oform-btn--primary) {
      background: ButtonText;
      color: ButtonFace;
      border: 2px solid ButtonText;
    }

    :where(.card, .panel, .modal, .dialog, .work-card, .blog-card) {
      border: 2px solid CanvasText;
      box-shadow: none;
    }

    /* 隐藏装饰性元素 */
    :where(
      .hero-bg-orb,
      .hero-aperture,
      .hero-bg-grain,
      .hero-bg-grid,
      .manuscript-grid-pattern,
      .blog-page::before,
      .work-page::before,
      .not-found-page::before
    ) {
      display: none !important;
    }

    /* 焦点 */
    :focus-visible {
      outline: 3px solid Highlight;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }

    /* 移除透明背景 */
    :where(.glass, .glass-soft, .glass-strong, .nav-pill, .mbn) {
      background: Canvas !important;
      backdrop-filter: none !important;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 对比度偏好：更高[reference:16] ─── */
  @media (prefers-contrast: more) {
    :root {
      --line: color-mix(in oklch, var(--ink) 35%, transparent);
      --line-strong: color-mix(in oklch, var(--ink) 55%, transparent);
      --ink-3: color-mix(in oklch, var(--ink) 85%, transparent);
    }

    :where(p, li, td, dd, .body) {
      color: var(--ink);
    }

    :where(a:not([class]), .blog-post-content a) {
      text-decoration: underline;
      text-underline-offset: 3px;
      text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
    }

    :where(.button, .btn, input, textarea, select, .card, .panel) {
      border-width: 2px;
    }

    :where(button, [role="button"], .button) {
      outline: 1px solid currentColor;
      outline-offset: 1px;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 对比度偏好：更低 ─── */
  @media (prefers-contrast: less) {
    :root {
      --line: color-mix(in oklch, var(--ink) 5%, transparent);
      --line-strong: color-mix(in oklch, var(--ink) 12%, transparent);
    }
  }

  /* ─── 透明度偏好：减少 ─── */
  @media (prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce) {
    :where(
      .glass,
      .glass-soft,
      .glass-strong,
      .nav-pill,
      .mbn,
      .welcome-backdrop,
      .project-modal,
      .hero-glass,
      .blog-inline-cta,
      .blog-funnel
    ) {
      backdrop-filter: none !important;
      -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
      background: var(--surface) !important;
      border: 1px solid var(--line);
    }
  }

  /* ─── 数据偏好：减少 ─── */
  @media (prefers-reduced-data: reduce) {
    :where(
      .hero-bg-orb,
      .hero-aperture,
      .hero-bg-grain,
      .hero-bg-grid,
      .manuscript-grid-pattern,
      .blog-page::before,
      .work-page::before,
      .not-found-page::before
    ) {
      display: none !important;
    }

    img {
      image-rendering: optimizeSpeed;
      content-visibility: auto;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 色彩空间偏好：P3 ─── */
  @media (color-gamut: p3) {
    :root {
      --accent: oklch(0.62 0.24 40); /* 更鲜艳的 P3 色彩 */
    }
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   7. 现代布局 — 容器查询、:has()、逻辑属性
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   组件响应父容器宽度，而非视口[reference:17][reference:18]。
   :has() 实现父级条件样式[reference:19]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer layout {
  /* ─── 容器查询基础 ─── */
  .card,
  .work-card,
  .blog-card,
  .commitment-card,
  .process-step,
  .testimonial-card,
  .service-item {
    container-type: inline-size;
    container-name: card;
  }

  /* ─── 卡片在窄容器中 ─── */
  @container card (max-width: 400px) {
    :where(.card, .work-card, .blog-card, .commitment-card) {
      padding: var(--space-2);
    }
  }

  /* ─── 卡片在宽容器中 ─── */
  @container card (min-width: 600px) {
    :where(.card, .work-card, .blog-card) {
      padding: var(--space-4);
    }

    :where(.work-card-title, .blog-card-title, .card-title) {
      font-size: 1.25rem;
    }
  }

  /* ─── :has() 条件样式 ─── */
  .form-field:has(input:focus) {
    --field-focus: 1;
  }

  .form-field:has(input:invalid:not(:placeholder-shown)) {
    --field-error: 1;
  }

  .form-field:has(input:valid:not(:placeholder-shown)) {
    --field-valid: 1;
  }

  /* ─── 有图片的卡片 → 溢出隐藏 ─── */
  .work-card:has(img),
  .blog-card:has(img),
  .portfolio-card:has(img) {
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* ─── 空列表容器 ─── */
  :where(ul, ol, .grid, .list):not(:has(li, .item, .card))::after {
    content: "هیچ موردی برای نمایش وجود ندارد";
    display: block;
    padding: var(--space-4);
    text-align: center;
    color: var(--ink-3);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    font-style: italic;
  }

  /* ─── 逻辑属性优先（RTL 原生支持）[reference:20] ─── */
  .container {
    inline-size: min(calc(100% - var(--space-4) * 2), var(--container));
    margin-inline: auto;
  }

  .container-narrow {
    inline-size: min(calc(100% - var(--space-4) * 2), var(--container-narrow));
    margin-inline: auto;
  }

  /* ─── 网格间距使用逻辑属性 ─── */
  :where(.grid, .grid-2, .grid-3, .grid-4) {
    display: grid;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  /* ─── 边距使用块轴属性 ─── */
  :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6) {
    margin-block-start: 0;
    margin-block-end: var(--space-2);
  }

  :where(p, ul, ol, blockquote, figure, pre, table) {
    margin-block-end: var(--space-3);
  }

  /* ─── 内边距使用逻辑属性 ─── */
  :where(.button, .btn) {
    padding-block: 0.75rem;
    padding-inline: 1.5rem;
  }

  /* ─── 边框使用逻辑属性 ─── */
  .callout,
  .aside,
  .note {
    border-inline-start: 3px solid var(--accent);
    padding-inline-start: var(--space-3);
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   8. 视图过渡与滚动驱动动画
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   View Transitions API 和 scroll-driven animations 是现代
   页面过渡和滚动效果的基石[reference:21]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer utilities {
  /* ─── 视图过渡（跨页面） ─── */
  @view-transition {
    navigation: auto;
  }

  ::view-transition-old(root) {
    animation: fade-out 200ms var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  ::view-transition-new(root) {
    animation: fade-in 240ms var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  @keyframes fade-out {
    from { opacity: 1; }
    to { opacity: 0; }
  }

  @keyframes fade-in {
    from { opacity: 0; }
    to { opacity: 1; }
  }

  /* ─── 滚动驱动动画（渐进增强） ─── */
  @supports (animation-timeline: view()) {
    .scroll-fade-in {
      animation: scroll-fade-in linear both;
      animation-timeline: view();
      animation-range: entry 0% cover 40%;
    }

    .scroll-slide-up {
      animation: scroll-slide-up linear both;
      animation-timeline: view();
      animation-range: entry 0% cover 50%;
    }

    @keyframes scroll-fade-in {
      from { opacity: 0; }
      to { opacity: 1; }
    }

    @keyframes scroll-slide-up {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(24px);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
    }
  }

  /* ─── 回退：使用 IntersectionObserver（已有 .reveal 系统） ─── */
  @supports not (animation-timeline: view()) {
    .scroll-fade-in,
    .scroll-slide-up {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: none;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 减少动态效果时的回退 ─── */
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    ::view-transition-old(root),
    ::view-transition-new(root) {
      animation: none;
    }

    .scroll-fade-in,
    .scroll-slide-up {
      animation: none !important;
      opacity: 1 !important;
      transform: none !important;
    }
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   9. 打印样式
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   专业的打印输出，A4 优化，隐藏装饰性元素。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer utilities {
  @media print {
    @page {
      margin: 2cm;
      size: A4 portrait;
    }

    /* ─── 隐藏非内容元素 ─── */
    :where(
      .site-nav,
      .mbn,
      .chapter-rail,
      .back-to-top,
      .sound-toggle,
      .mobile-nav,
      .footer-masthead,
      .footer-hero,
      .footer-bottom-wrap,
      .welcome-overlay,
      .page-transition,
      .scroll-progress,
      .reading-progress,
      .toast-stack,
      .hero-bg,
      .hero-marquee,
      .hero-ruler,
      .hero-crosshair,
      .signature-ink,
      .hero-aperture,
      .hero-glass,
      .blog-inline-cta,
      .blog-funnel,
      .blog-share,
      .project-modal,
      .oform-actions
    ) {
      display: none !important;
    }

    /* ─── 基础排版 ─── */
    html,
    body {
      background: #fff !important;
      color: #000 !important;
      font-size: 11pt !important;
      line-height: 1.5 !important;
    }

    :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6) {
      color: #000 !important;
      page-break-after: avoid;
      break-after: avoid;
    }

    :where(p, li, blockquote) {
      color: #222 !important;
      orphans: 3;
      widows: 3;
    }

    /* ─── 链接 URL 显示 ─── */
    :where(a) {
      color: #000 !important;
      text-decoration: underline !important;
    }

    :where(a[href^="http"])::after {
      content: " (" attr(href) ")";
      font-size: 0.85em;
      color: #555;
      word-break: break-all;
    }

    /* ─── 按钮作为文本 ─── */
    :where(.button, .btn) {
      border: 1px solid #000 !important;
      background: transparent !important;
      color: #000 !important;
      box-shadow: none !important;
    }

    /* ─── 卡片和面板 ─── */
    :where(.card, .panel, .work-card, .blog-card, .commitment-card) {
      border: 1px solid #ccc !important;
      box-shadow: none !important;
      page-break-inside: avoid;
      break-inside: avoid;
    }

    /* ─── 图片和图表 ─── */
    :where(img, figure) {
      max-inline-size: 100% !important;
      page-break-inside: avoid;
      break-inside: avoid;
    }

    /* ─── 隐藏动画和过渡 ─── */
    * {
      animation: none !important;
      transition: none !important;
    }
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   10. 国际化与双向文本（RTL 最佳实践）
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   波斯语文本永远不应应用 letter-spacing[reference:22]。
   代码和 LTR 内容使用 bidi isolate[reference:23]。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  /* ─── 波斯语文本：无 letter-spacing ─── */
  :where([lang="fa"], [lang="ar"], [lang="ur"], [dir="rtl"]) {
    letter-spacing: 0;
    word-spacing: normal;
  }

  /* ─── 拉丁语文本：可以使用 letter-spacing ─── */
  :where([lang="en"], [lang="la"]) {
    letter-spacing: normal;
  }

  /* ─── 混合方向内容 ─── */
  .ltr-content {
    direction: ltr;
    unicode-bidi: isolate;
  }

  .rtl-content {
    direction: rtl;
    unicode-bidi: isolate;
  }

  /* ─── 数字：波斯语数字使用 tabular-nums ─── */
  .persian-nums {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    direction: ltr;
    unicode-bidi: isolate;
    font-feature-settings: "tnum" 1;
  }

  /* ─── 拉丁数字 ─── */
  .latin-nums {
    direction: ltr;
    unicode-bidi: isolate;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums;
  }

  /* ─── 代码块始终 LTR ─── */
  :where(pre, code, kbd, samp) {
    direction: ltr !important;
    unicode-bidi: isolate !important;
    text-align: left !important;
  }

  /* ─── 表单输入：根据内容方向自动切换 ─── */
  :where(input[type="email"], input[type="url"], input[type="tel"], input[type="number"]) {
    direction: ltr;
    text-align: start;
  }

  /* ─── 双向文本的容器 ─── */
  .bidi-container {
    unicode-bidi: plaintext;
  }

  /* ─── 波斯语 Typography 优化 ─── */
  :where([lang="fa"]) :where(p, li, dd, blockquote) {
    line-height: 1.85; /* 波斯语需要更高的行高 */
    text-wrap: pretty;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   11. 微细节 — 选区、光标、滚动条
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   这些细节用户不会注意到，但缺少它们会感觉“不对”。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer base {
  /* ─── 选区 ─── */
  ::selection {
    background: color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 85%, transparent);
    color: #fff;
    text-shadow: none;
  }

  ::-moz-selection {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: #fff;
  }

  /* ─── 光标 ─── */
  :where(input, textarea, [contenteditable]) {
    caret-color: var(--accent);
  }

  /* ─── 滚动条（WebKit） ─── */
  ::-webkit-scrollbar {
    inline-size: 10px;
    block-size: 10px;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
    background: transparent;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background: linear-gradient(
      180deg,
      color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 65%, transparent) 0%,
      color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 40%, transparent) 100%
    );
    border-radius: 10px;
    border: 3px solid transparent;
    background-clip: content-box;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--accent) 0%, var(--accent-2) 100%);
    background-clip: content-box;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-corner {
    background: transparent;
  }

  /* ─── 滚动条（Firefox） ─── */
  * {
    scrollbar-width: thin;
    scrollbar-color: color-mix(in oklch, var(--accent) 55%, transparent) transparent;
  }

  /* ─── 图片渲染优化 ─── */
  img {
    max-inline-size: 100%;
    block-size: auto;
    image-rendering: -webkit-optimize-contrast;
  }

  img[loading="lazy"] {
    content-visibility: auto;
  }

  /* ─── Placeholder ─── */
  ::placeholder {
    color: var(--ink-muted);
    opacity: 0.8;
  }

  /* ─── 禁用状态 ─── */
  :where(button, input, textarea, select):disabled {
    opacity: 0.5;
    cursor: not-allowed;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  /* ─── 自动填充修复（Chrome 黄色背景） ─── */
  input:-webkit-autofill,
  input:-webkit-autofill:hover,
  input:-webkit-autofill:focus,
  textarea:-webkit-autofill,
  textarea:-webkit-autofill:hover,
  textarea:-webkit-autofill:focus,
  select:-webkit-autofill {
    -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--ink);
    -webkit-box-shadow: 0 0 0 1000px var(--surface) inset;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1000px var(--surface) inset;
    transition: background-color 5000s ease-in-out 0s;
  }

  /* ─── 暗色模式自动填充 ─── */
  [data-theme="dark"] input:-webkit-autofill,
  [data-theme="dark"] textarea:-webkit-autofill {
    -webkit-box-shadow: 0 0 0 1000px var(--surface) inset;
    -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--ink);
  }

  /* ─── 锚点滚动 ─── */
  :target {
    scroll-margin-block-start: 6rem;
  }

  /* ─── 触摸优化 ─── */
  :where(button, a, [role="button"], input, textarea, select) {
    touch-action: manipulation;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  }

  /* ─── 内容可见性（性能优化） ─── */
  :where(section, footer, .section) {
    content-visibility: auto;
    contain-intrinsic-size: auto 800px;
  }

  :where(.hero-section) {
    content-visibility: visible;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   12. 性能优化 — GPU 合成与层管理
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   确保所有动画使用 transform 和 opacity（仅合成层）。
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@layer utilities {
  /* ─── 合成层提升 ─── */
  :where(
    .hero-bg-orb,

  }

  :where(.button, .btn, a, .card, .work-card, .blog-card) {
    transition-property: transform, opacity, box-shadow, background-color, border-color, color;
    transition-duration: var(--dur-fast);
    transition-timing-function: var(--ease-out-expo);
  }

  /* ─── 布局包容性（桌面端） ─── */

      contain: layout style paint;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 移动端：禁用重型效果 ─── */

    }

      .blog-card:hover,
      .commitment-card:hover,
      .process-step:hover,
      .testimonial-card:hover,
      .faq-item:hover
    ) {
      transform: none !important;
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm) !important;
    }
  }

  /* ─── 标签页隐藏时暂停动画 ─── */
  html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *,
  html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *::before,
  html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *::after {
    animation-play-state: paused !important;
  }

    }

/* ============================================================
   PERFORMANCE LAYER — Last-Mile Optimization
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   This is NOT a mega-file. It's a surgical override layer
   that touches only what matters for performance, without
   adding new visual rules.

   Strategy:
   1. Content-visibility for below-fold sections
   2. Reduce blur radius (biggest GPU cost)
   3. Reduce box-shadow complexity
   4. Remove unused animations
   5. Clean will-change
   6. Ensure compositor-only animations
   7. Section containment strategy

   Load LAST. No !important unless fighting legacy overrides.
   ============================================================ */

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   1. CONTENT-VISIBILITY — skip rendering off-screen
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.services-section,
.portfolio-section,
.about-section,
.why-section,
.testimonials-section,
.latest-blog-section,
.newsletter-section,
.faq-section,
.final-cta-section,
.commitments-section,
.process-section {
  content-visibility: auto;
  contain-intrinsic-size: auto 900px;
}

.site-footer {
  content-visibility: auto;
  contain-intrinsic-size: auto 700px;
}

/* Hero must stay visible always */
.hero-section {
  content-visibility: visible !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   2. BLUR REDUCTION — the biggest GPU cost on the site
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Hero orbs — 100px → 80px is barely visible but 40% cheaper */
.hero-section .hero-bg-orb,
.hero-aurora-blob {
  filter: blur(80px);
}

/* Hide the third orb on mobile (has the least visual impact) */
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .hero-section .hero-bg-orb-3 {
    display: none;
  }

  .hero-section .hero-bg-orb-2 {
    opacity: 0.35;
  }
}

/* Reduce nav glass blur on mobile (costly on iOS) */
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .site-nav.is-scrolled .nav-inner,
  .mobile-nav {
    backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(1.3);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(14px) saturate(1.3);
  }
}

/* Reduce hero bento cell blur (from 26px → 20px) */
@media (min-width: 900px) {
  .hero-section .hero-bento-cell {
    backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(1.5);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(1.5);
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   3. BOX-SHADOW SIMPLIFICATION
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Complex multi-layer shadows cost paint time.
   Merging into fewer layers keeps the same look.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.hero-section .hero-bento-cell {
  box-shadow:
    inset 0 1px 0 color-mix(in srgb, #fff 55%, transparent),
    0 12px 32px -16px rgba(10, 9, 8, 0.12);
}

.commitment-card,
.process-step,
.latest-blog-card,
.faq-item,
.work-card {
  box-shadow:
    inset 0 1px 0 color-mix(in srgb, #fff 40%, transparent),
    0 1px 2px rgba(10, 9, 8, 0.04);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   4. PAUSE OFF-SCREEN ANIMATIONS
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Infinite CSS animations consume GPU even when off-screen.
   We pause the costly ones when their section isn't visible.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Marquee — only animate when Hero is in view */
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-marquee-track {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

/* Bento Live dot — pause when off screen */
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-bento-live-dot,
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-bento-status-dot {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

/* Orbs — pause when Hero isn't visible */
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-bg-orb {
  animation-play-state: paused;
}

/* Tab hidden — pause everything */
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .hero-marquee-track,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .hero-bg-orb,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .hero-bento-live-dot,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .hero-bento-status-dot,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .hero-aperture,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] .chapter-rail-item.is-active .chapter-rail-dot {
  animation-play-state: paused !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   5. WILL-CHANGE CLEANUP
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   will-change should only exist during active animation.
   Leaving it on permanently creates pointless GPU layers.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.reveal,
.reveal-fade,
.reveal-scale,
.hero-bento-cell,
.hero-bento-seal,
.about-brand-mark,
.newsletter-card,
.commitment-card,
.process-step,
.latest-blog-card,
.faq-item {
  will-change: auto;
}

/* Only hint GPU during actual reveal */
.reveal:not(.is-visible) {
  will-change: opacity, transform;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   6. COMPOSITOR-ONLY ANIMATIONS
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Force the few remaining infinite animations to run on the
   compositor thread (transform/opacity only).
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.hero-marquee-track,
.hero-bg-orb,
.hero-bento-live-dot,
.hero-bento-status-dot,
.hero-aperture,
.hero-section .hero-bento-cell-mesh::before,
.chapter-rail-item.is-active .chapter-rail-dot {
  transform: translateZ(0);
  backface-visibility: hidden;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   7. REDUCE HOVER TRANSFORM COST ON MOBILE
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   On touch devices, hover effects never trigger meaningfully.
   Kill them to save GPU cycles.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@media (hover: none), (pointer: coarse) {
  .portfolio-card:hover .portfolio-card-visual,
  .work-card:hover,
  .latest-blog-card:hover,
  .faq-item:hover,
  .commitment-card:hover,
  .testimonial-card:hover,
  .service-item:hover {
    transform: none;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   8. LAYOUT CONTAINMENT (Desktop only — safe)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */


/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   9. REDUCED MOTION — global safety
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .hero-marquee-track,

/* ============================================================
   PERFORMANCE PRO — Desktop Optimization Layer
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Goal: 40-60% reduction in GPU/CPU cost on desktop.
   No visual change. Loaded LAST so it wins all cascade.
   ============================================================ */

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   1. BACKDROP-FILTER REDUCTION — biggest GPU win
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Blur above ~20px is visually indistinguishable but roughly
   doubles GPU cost. We drop to 12-20px across the board.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@media (min-width: 901px) {
  /* Nav (scrolled) — 40px → 20px */
  .site-nav.is-scrolled .nav-inner {
    backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(1.6) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(1.6) !important;
  }

  /* Hero bento cells — 26px → 16px */
  .hero-section .hero-bento-cell {
    backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
  }

  /* Hero pills/eyebrows — 12px → 10px */
  .hero-section .hero-role,
  .hero-section .hero-top-meta,
  .hero-section .hero-eyebrow {
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px) saturate(1.2) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(10px) saturate(1.2) !important;
  }

  /* Service preview — 20px → 12px */
  .service-preview {
    backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
  }

  /* About statement — 20px → 12px */
  .about-statement {
    backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
  }

  /* Final CTA — 16px → 12px */
  .final-cta {
    backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(1.3) !important;
  }

  /* Order form sections — kill entirely (solid bg) */
  .order-page .form-section,
  .order-form .form-section {
    backdrop-filter: none !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
  }

  /* Modals — 24px → 16px is more than enough */
  .project-modal-shell,
  .project-modal {
    backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
  }

  .welcome-backdrop,
  .welcome-card {
    backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(1.4) !important;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   2. KILL BACKDROP-FILTER WHERE IT DOES NOTHING
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   These have solid/near-solid backgrounds — blur has no visible
   effect but still forces an extra compositing pass per frame.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.contact-card,
.commitment-card,
.process-step,
.latest-blog-card,
.faq-item,
.testimonial-card,
.work-card,
.newsletter-card,
.blog-author,
.blog-inline-cta,
.blog-funnel,
.service-item,
.hero-section .hero-bento-seal,
.site-footer .footer-col,
.order-page .form-field input,
.order-page .form-field textarea {
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* Desktop nav list — has solid-ish gradient, blur not needed */
.site-nav .desktop-nav-list {
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   3. PAUSE ANIMATIONS WHEN OFF-SCREEN
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   data-perf-pause is set by PerfObserver (JS component).
   Infinite animations burn GPU every frame, even off-screen.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
[data-perf-pause="true"],
[data-perf-pause="true"] *,
[data-perf-pause="true"] *::before,
[data-perf-pause="true"] *::after {
  animation-play-state: paused !important;
}

/* Pause everything when tab is hidden */
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *::before,
html[data-tab-hidden="true"] *::after {
  animation-play-state: paused !important;
}

/* Hero-specific pause (existing pattern, extended) */
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-bg-orb,
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-bg-grid,
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-marquee-track,

.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-eyebrow-dot,
.hero-section:not(.hero-in-view) .hero-sig-path {
  animation-play-state: paused !important;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   4. WILL-CHANGE CLEANUP
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Permanent will-change hints waste GPU memory (each one
   allocates a full-size texture). Hint only during animation.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.reveal,
.reveal-fade,
.reveal-scale,
.hero-bento-cell,
.commitment-card,
.process-step,
.latest-blog-card,
.faq-item,
.testimonial-card,
.newsletter-card,
.service-preview,
.about-statement {
  will-change: auto;
}

/* Only hint GPU during reveal-in-progress */
.reveal:not(.is-visible) {
  will-change: opacity, transform;
}

/* Composited-layer hints for infinite animations */
.hero-bg-orb,
.hero-marquee-track,
.hero-bento-seal,
.hero-masthead-dot,
.hero-bento-status-dot,
.hero-bento-live-dot,
.hero-section .hero-bento-cell-mesh::before {
  transform: translateZ(0);
  backface-visibility: hidden;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   5. LAYOUT CONTAINMENT — desktop only
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   `contain: layout style` tells the browser style recalc and
   layout are scoped to the subtree. Safe — doesn't affect
   position: fixed/sticky children (that's `contain: paint`).
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */


/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   6. SHADOW SIMPLIFICATION
   ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   Multi-layer shadows cause extra paint passes on every frame
   they change. Simplify to 2-layer where possible.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@media (min-width: 901px) {
  .commitment-card,

  .latest-blog-card,

}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import MagneticButton from "@/components/MagneticButton";
import EditorialImage from "@/components/ui/EditorialImage";
import LiveClock from "@/components/ui/LiveClock";

type LivePost = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  date: string;
};

type HeroProps = {
  latestPost: LivePost | null;
  archivePost: LivePost | null;
  totalPosts: number;
  totalProjects: number;
};

const LIVE_ROTATION_MS = 5500;

const MARQUEE_WORDS = [
  "WEB DESIGN",
  "CREATIVE DEVELOPMENT",
  "AI VISUALS",
  "BRAND IDENTITY",
  "MOTION",
  "INTERFACE",
];

const PERSIAN_DIGITS = "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹";

function toPersian(value: string | number): string {
  return String(value).replace(/\d/g, (d) => PERSIAN_DIGITS[+d]);
}

const CURRENT_PERSIAN_YEAR = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
  year: "numeric",
})
  .format(new Date())
  .replace(/[^\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, "")
  .slice(0, 4);

function usePersianDate() {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [date, setDate] = useState({
    day: "--",
    month: "--",
    year: "----",
    weekday: "--",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    const update = () => {
      const now = new Date();
      const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
        day: "numeric",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
        weekday: "long",
        timeZone: "Asia/Tehran",
      });
      const parts = fmt.formatToParts(now);
      const get = (t: string) => parts.find((p) => p.type === t)?.value ?? "--";
      setDate({
        day: get("day"),
        month: get("month"),
        year: get("year").replace(/[^\u06F0-\u06F9]/g, ""),
        weekday: get("weekday"),
      });
    };
    update();
    const id = window.setInterval(update, 30000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  return mounted
    ? date
    : { day: "--", month: "--", year: "----", weekday: "--" };
}

function useRelativeTime(iso: string | null): string {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    if (!iso) return;
    const compute = () => {
      const diff = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
      const days = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
      if (days <= 0) setText("امروز");
      else if (days === 1) setText("دیروز");
      else if (days < 30) setText(`${toPersian(days)} روز پیش`);
      else if (days < 365)
        setText(`${toPersian(Math.floor(days / 30))} ماه پیش`);
      else setText(`${toPersian(Math.floor(days / 365))} سال پیش`);
    };
    compute();
    const id = window.setInterval(compute, 120000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [iso]);

  return mounted ? text : "";
}

export default function Hero({
  latestPost,
  archivePost,
  totalPosts,
  totalProjects,
}: HeroProps) {
  const sectionRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);

  const [liveIndex, setLiveIndex] = useState(0);
  const [livePaused, setLivePaused] = useState(false);
  const [heroInView, setHeroInView] = useState(true);
  const [tabVisible, setTabVisible] = useState(true);

  const persianDate = usePersianDate();
  const latestAgo = useRelativeTime(latestPost?.date ?? null);
  const archiveAgo = useRelativeTime(archivePost?.date ?? null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;
    const section = sectionRef.current;
    if (!section) return;

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => setHeroInView(entry.isIntersecting),
      { threshold: 0.1 }
    );
    io.observe(section);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onVis = () => {
      setTabVisible(!document.hidden);
      document.documentElement.setAttribute(
        "data-tab-hidden",
        document.hidden ? "true" : "false"
      );
    };
    onVis();
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const section = sectionRef.current;
    if (!section) return;
    if (heroInView) section.classList.add("hero-in-view");
    else section.classList.remove("hero-in-view");
  }, [heroInView]);

  const shouldAnimate = heroInView && tabVisible;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (livePaused || !shouldAnimate) return;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)"
    ).matches;
    if (reduced) return;
    const id = window.setInterval(
      () => setLiveIndex((i) => (i + 1) % 3),
      LIVE_ROTATION_MS
    );
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [livePaused, shouldAnimate]);

  const liveStates = useMemo(() => {
    const states: Array<{
      id: "latest" | "stats" | "archive";
      render: () => React.ReactNode;
    }> = [];

    if (latestPost) {
      states.push({
        id: "latest",
        render: () => (
          <Link
            href={`/blog/${latestPost.slug}`}
            prefetch={false}
            className="hero-bento-live-item"
          >
            <span className="hero-bento-live-label">
              NOW{" "}
              <span className="hero-bento-live-label-text">
                · آخرین یادداشت
              </span>
            </span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-title">
              {latestPost.title}
            </span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-arrow" aria-hidden="true">
              ←
            </span>
            {latestAgo && (
              <span className="hero-bento-live-ago">{latestAgo}</span>
            )}
          </Link>
        ),
      });
    }

    states.push({
      id: "stats",
      render: () => (
        <Link
          href="/blog"
          prefetch={false}
          className="hero-bento-live-item hero-bento-live-item--stats"
        >
          <span className="hero-bento-live-label">
            INDEX{" "}
            <span className="hero-bento-live-label-text">· آمار زنده</span>
          </span>
          <span className="hero-bento-live-stats">
            <span className="hero-bento-live-stat">
              <strong>{toPersian(totalPosts)}</strong>
              <span>مقاله</span>
            </span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-stat-sep">·</span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-stat">
              <strong>{toPersian(totalProjects)}</strong>
              <span>پروژه</span>
            </span>
          </span>
        </Link>
      ),
    });

    if (archivePost) {
      states.push({
        id: "archive",
        render: () => (
          <Link
            href={`/blog/${archivePost.slug}`}
            prefetch={false}
            className="hero-bento-live-item"
          >
            <span className="hero-bento-live-label">
              ARCHIVE{" "}
              <span className="hero-bento-live-label-text">
                · از آرشیو
              </span>
            </span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-title">
              {archivePost.title}
            </span>
            <span className="hero-bento-live-arrow" aria-hidden="true">
              ←
            </span>
            {archiveAgo && (
              <span className="hero-bento-live-ago">{archiveAgo}</span>
            )}
          </Link>
        ),
      });
    }

    return states;
  }, [
    latestPost,
    archivePost,
    latestAgo,
    archiveAgo,
    totalPosts,
    totalProjects,
  ]);

  const activeLive = liveStates[liveIndex % liveStates.length];

  return (
    <section
      ref={sectionRef}
      id="home"
      className="hero-section hero-in-view"
      data-hero
    >
      <div className="hero-bg" aria-hidden="true">
        <span className="hero-bg-orb hero-bg-orb-1" />
        <span className="hero-bg-orb hero-bg-orb-2" />
        <span className="hero-bg-orb hero-bg-orb-3" />
        <span className="hero-bg-grid" />
        <span className="hero-bg-grain" />
      </div>

      <div className="hero-ruler hero-ruler-top" aria-hidden="true">
        {Array.from({ length: 13 }).map((_, i) => (
          <span key={i}>{toPersian(String(i).padStart(2, "0"))}</span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="hero-ruler hero-ruler-left" aria-hidden="true">
        {["A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G", "H"].map((l) => (
          <span key={l}>{l}</span>
        ))}
      </div>

      <span className="hero-crosshair hero-crosshair-tl" aria-hidden="true">
        <CrosshairMark />
      </span>
      <span className="hero-crosshair hero-crosshair-tr" aria-hidden="true">
        <CrosshairMark />
      </span>
      <span className="hero-crosshair hero-crosshair-bl" aria-hidden="true">
        <CrosshairMark />

export default function Services() {
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const tabRefs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);
  const current = services[active];

  const focusTab = (index: number) => {
    setActive(index);
    tabRefs.current[index]?.focus();
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent, index: number) => {
    const last = services.length - 1;
    let nextIndex = index;

    if (event.key === "ArrowDown" || event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
      event.preventDefault();
      nextIndex = index === last ? 0 : index + 1;
    } else if (event.key === "ArrowUp" || event.key === "ArrowRight") {
      event.preventDefault();
      nextIndex = index === 0 ? last : index - 1;
    } else if (event.key === "Home") {
      event.preventDefault();
      nextIndex = 0;
    } else if (event.key === "End") {
      event.preventDefault();
      nextIndex = last;
    } else {
      return;
    }

    focusTab(nextIndex);
  };

  const activeNumber = String(active + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const totalNumber = String(services.length).padStart(2, "0");

  return (
    <section id="services" className="services-section section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="services-masthead reveal">
          <span className="services-masthead-left">
            CHAPTER&nbsp;·&nbsp;01&nbsp;—&nbsp;SERVICES
          </span>
          <span className="services-masthead-center" aria-hidden="true">
            §
          </span>
          <span className="services-masthead-right">
            SELECTED&nbsp;·&nbsp;{CURRENT_PERSIAN_YEAR}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="section-heading reveal">
          <div>
            <SectionNumber num="۰۱" label="SERVICES" />
            <h2>
              از ایده تا یک
              <em> حضور دیجیتالِ دقیق</em>
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p>
            چهار مسیر برای ساختن چیزی که فقط زیبا نیست — کار می‌کنه. هر
            خدمت با هدف مشخص، زمان مشخص و نتیجه‌ی قابل اندازه‌گیری.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="services-editorial reveal">
          <EditorialImage
            src="/home-services.webp"
            alt="فضای کاری"
            aspectRatio="21 / 9"
            radius="xl"
            overlay="bottom"
            sizes="(max-width: 720px) 100vw, 1280px"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="services-layout">
          <div
            className="services-list reveal"
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="فهرست خدمات"
            aria-orientation="vertical"
          >
            <div className="services-list-header" aria-hidden="true">
              <span className="services-list-header-label">INDEX</span>
              <span className="services-list-header-count">
                {activeNumber}&nbsp;/&nbsp;{totalNumber}
              </span>
            </div>

            {services.map((service, index) => (
              <button
                key={service.number}
                ref={(el) => {
                  tabRefs.current[index] = el;
                }}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                id={`service-tab-${index}`}
                aria-selected={active === index}
                aria-controls={`service-panel-${index}`}
                tabIndex={active === index ? 0 : -1}
                className={
                  "service-item" + (active === index ? " is-active" : "")
                }
                onClick={() => setActive(index)}
                onKeyDown={(event) => handleKeyDown(event, index)}
              >
                <span className="service-item-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="service-number">{service.number}</span>
                <span className="service-icon" aria-hidden="true">
                  <ServiceIconLarge type={service.icon} />
                </span>
                <span className="service-title">{service.shortTitle}</span>
                <span className="service-arrow" aria-hidden="true">
                  ↙
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div
            className="service-preview reveal"
            role="tabpanel"
            id={`service-panel-${active}`}
            aria-labelledby={`service-tab-${active}`}
          >
            <span
              className="service-preview-corner service-preview-corner-tl"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <span
              className="service-preview-corner service-preview-corner-tr"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <span
              className="service-preview-corner service-preview-corner-bl"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <span
              className="service-preview-corner service-preview-corner-br"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />

            <div className="service-preview-top">
              <span>
                {current.number} /{" "}
                {String(services.length).padStart(2, "0")}
              </span>
              <span>SELECTED SERVICE</span>
            </div>

            <div className="service-preview-content">
              <span className="service-preview-icon" aria-hidden="true">
                <ServiceIconLarge type={current.icon} />
              </span>

              <span className="service-preview-label">
                {current.shortTitle}
              </span>

              <h3>{current.title}</h3>

              <p>{current.description}</p>

              <ul className="service-features">
                {current.features.map((feature) => (
                  <li key={feature} className="service-feature">
                    {feature}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>

              <div className="service-meta">
                <div className="service-meta-item">
                  <span>تحویل</span>
                  <strong>{current.deliverables}</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="service-meta-item">
                  <span>زمان تقریبی</span>
                  <strong>{current.timeline}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="service-tags">
                {current.tags.map((tag) => (
                  <span key={tag}>{tag}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="service-visual" aria-hidden="true">
              <span className="service-visual-numeral">
                {current.number}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { testimonials } from "@/content/testimonials";
import SectionNumber from "@/components/SectionNumber";

export default function Testimonials() {
  // Build Review schema for SEO
  const reviewSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "ItemList",
    itemListElement: testimonials.map((t, index) => ({
      "@type": "ListItem",
      position: index + 1,
      item: {
        "@type": "Review",
        author: {
          "@type": "Person",
          name: t.name,
        },
        reviewBody: t.text,
        reviewRating: {
          "@type": "Rating",
          ratingValue: t.rating,
          bestRating: 5,
        },
        itemReviewed: {
          "@type": "LocalBusiness",
          "name": "امیرحسین شرکائی | طراحی سایت اختصاصی",
          "image": "https://shorakaei.ir/icon.webp",
          "url": "https://shorakaei.ir"
        },
      },
    })),
  };

  return (
    <section className="testimonials-section section" aria-labelledby="testimonials-title">
      <script
        type="application/ld+json"
        dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(reviewSchema) }}
      />
      <div className="container">
        <div className="section-heading reveal">
          <div>
            <SectionNumber num="۰۶" label="TESTIMONIALS" />
            <h2 id="testimonials-title">
              نظر مشتریان
              <em className="ink-word"> درباره همکاری با من</em>
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p>
            اعتماد شما بزرگ‌ترین سرمایه من است. این‌ها بخشی از تجربه‌های
            کسانی است که با من همکاری کرده‌اند.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="testimonials-grid">
          {testimonials.map((t) => (
            <div key={t.id} className="testimonial-card reveal">
              <div className="testimonial-stars" aria-label={`امتیاز ${t.rating} از 5`}>
                {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <span key={i} aria-hidden="true">
                    {i < t.rating ? "★" : "☆"}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
              <p className="testimonial-text">«{t.text}»</p>
              <div className="testimonial-author">
                {t.avatar && (
                  <Image
                    src={t.avatar}
                    alt={t.name}
                    width={48}
                    height={48}
                    className="testimonial-avatar"
                  />
                )}
                <div>
                  <strong>{t.name}</strong>
                  <br />
                  <small>
                    {t.role} — {t.company}
                  </small>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="testimonials-footer reveal">
          <Link href="/order" className="button button-primary">
            شروع همکاری با من
            <span aria-hidden="true">←</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   MOTION SYSTEM — 2026 Global Standards
   ------------------------------------------------------------
   - Physics-based easings (spring, bounce, anticipate)
   - Scroll-driven animations with @supports fallback
   - View Transitions API for route navigation
   - Every layer respects prefers-reduced-motion
   ============================================================ */

:root {
  /* ═══ DURATION SCALE — T-shirt sizing ═══ */
  --motion-instant:   80ms;
  --motion-fast:      160ms;
  --motion-base:      240ms;
  --motion-medium:    320ms;
  --motion-slow:      480ms;
  --motion-slower:    640ms;
  --motion-cinematic: 900ms;

  /* ═══ EASING CURVES ═══ */
  --motion-ease-out:        cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  --motion-ease-in-out:     cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1);
  --motion-ease-emphasis:   cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
  --motion-ease-spring:     cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
  --motion-ease-bounce:     cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.55, 0.265, 1.55);
  --motion-ease-anticipate: cubic-bezier(0.36, 0, 0.66, -0.56);
  --motion-ease-sharp:      cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  --motion-ease-cinematic:  cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1);

  /* ═══ COMPOSITE PRESETS ═══ */
  --motion-button: var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease-out);
  --motion-card:   var(--motion-medium) var(--motion-ease-emphasis);
  --motion-modal:  var(--motion-medium) var(--motion-ease-emphasis);
  --motion-menu:   var(--motion-base) var(--motion-ease-emphasis);
  --motion-reveal: var(--motion-slow) var(--motion-ease-emphasis);

  /* ═══ DISTANCES ═══ */
  --motion-distance-xs: 4px;
  --motion-distance-sm: 8px;
  --motion-distance-md: 16px;
  --motion-distance-lg: 24px;
  --motion-distance-xl: 40px;

  /* ═══ BLUR VALUES ═══ */
  --motion-blur-sm: blur(2px);
  --motion-blur-md: blur(6px);
  --motion-blur-lg: blur(12px);
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   REDUCED MOTION — global safety net
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }

  .motion-slide-up,
  .motion-slide-down,
  .motion-scale-in,
  .motion-bounce-in {
    transform: none !important;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UTILITY ANIMATION CLASSES
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
.motion-fade {
  animation: motionFadeIn var(--motion-reveal);
}

.motion-slide-up {
  animation: motionSlideUp var(--motion-reveal);
}

.motion-slide-down {
  animation: motionSlideDown var(--motion-reveal);
}

.motion-scale-in {
  animation: motionScaleIn var(--motion-modal);
}

.motion-bounce-in {
  animation: motionBounceIn var(--motion-slow) var(--motion-ease-bounce);
}

.motion-spin {
  animation: motionSpin 1s linear infinite;
}

@keyframes motionFadeIn {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to   { opacity: 1; }
}

@keyframes motionSlideUp {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(var(--motion-distance-md));
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes motionSlideDown {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(calc(var(--motion-distance-md) * -1));
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes motionScaleIn {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: scale(0.95);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scale(1);
  }
}

@keyframes motionBounceIn {
  0%   { transform: scale(0.7); opacity: 0; }
  60%  { transform: scale(1.08); opacity: 1; }
  100% { transform: scale(1); }
}

@keyframes motionSpin {
  from { transform: rotate(0deg); }
  to   { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   SCROLL-DRIVEN ANIMATIONS
   Zero JavaScript. Runs on compositor thread.
   Guarded with @supports for progressive enhancement.
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@supports (animation-timeline: view()) {
  .scroll-fade-in {
    animation: scrollFadeIn linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 60%;
  }

  .scroll-slide-up {
    animation: scrollSlideUp linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 50%;
  }

  .scroll-slide-right {
    animation: scrollSlideRight linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 50%;
  }

  .scroll-slide-left {
    animation: scrollSlideLeft linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 50%;
  }

  .scroll-scale-in {
    animation: scrollScaleIn linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 10% entry 60%;
  }

  .scroll-blur-in {
    animation: scrollBlurIn linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 60%;
  }

  .scroll-parallax {
    animation: scrollParallax linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: cover;
  }

  @keyframes scrollFadeIn {
    from { opacity: 0; }
    to   { opacity: 1; }
  }

  @keyframes scrollSlideUp {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateY(var(--motion-distance-lg));
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: translateY(0);
    }
  }

  @keyframes scrollSlideRight {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateX(calc(var(--motion-distance-lg) * -1));
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: translateX(0);
    }
  }

  @keyframes scrollSlideLeft {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: translateX(var(--motion-distance-lg));
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: translateX(0);
    }
  }

  @keyframes scrollScaleIn {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      transform: scale(0.92);
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      transform: scale(1);
    }
  }

  @keyframes scrollBlurIn {
    from {
      opacity: 0;
      filter: var(--motion-blur-md);
    }
    to {
      opacity: 1;
      filter: blur(0);
    }
  }

  @keyframes scrollParallax {
    from { transform: translateY(-2%); }
    to   { transform: translateY(2%); }
  }

  /* Staggered children — reveal each child with delay */
  .scroll-stagger > * {
    animation: scrollSlideUp linear both;
    animation-timeline: view();
    animation-range: entry 0% entry 50%;
  }

  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(1) { animation-range: entry 0% entry 40%; }
  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(2) { animation-range: entry 5% entry 45%; }
  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(3) { animation-range: entry 10% entry 50%; }
  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(4) { animation-range: entry 15% entry 55%; }
  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(5) { animation-range: entry 20% entry 60%; }
  .scroll-stagger > *:nth-child(6) { animation-range: entry 25% entry 65%; }
}

/* Fallback for browsers without scroll-driven support */
@supports not (animation-timeline: view()) {
  .scroll-fade-in,
  .scroll-slide-up,
  .scroll-slide-right,
  .scroll-slide-left,
  .scroll-scale-in,
  .scroll-blur-in,
  .scroll-stagger > * {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: none;
    filter: none;
  }
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   VIEW TRANSITIONS — smooth route navigation
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
@view-transition {
  navigation: auto;
}

::view-transition-old(root) {
  animation: viewTransitionOut var(--motion-base) var(--motion-ease-out);
}

::view-transition-new(root) {
  animation: viewTransitionIn var(--motion-medium) var(--motion-ease-emphasis);
}

@keyframes viewTransitionOut {
  from { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
  to   { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-8px) scale(0.98); }
}

@keyframes viewTransitionIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(12px) scale(0.98); }
  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
}

/* Persistent elements across navigation */
.view-transition-persist-header {
  view-transition-name: header;
}

.view-transition-persist-footer {
  view-transition-name: footer;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  ::view-transition-old(root),
  ::view-transition-new(root) {
    animation: none;
  }
}
